function sha256OfFile(filePath: string): Promise<st
 return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
 const s = fs.createReadStream(filePath);
 s.on("data", (d) => h.update(d));
 s.on("error", reject);
 s.on("end", () => resolve(h.digest("hex")));
 });
}

async function sha256OfFile(filePath: string): Prom
 return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
 const s = fs.createReadStream(filePath);
 s.on("data", (d) => h.update(d));
 s.on("error", reject);
 s.on("end", () => resolve(h.digest("hex")));
 });
}

function getPrimaryKeyColumns(tableName: string): s

async function writeLine(ws: fs.WriteStream, line: 
 if (!ws.write(line)) await once(ws, "drain");

async function sha256OfFile(filePath: string): Prom
 return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
 const s = fs.createReadStream(filePath);
 s.on("data", (d) => h.update(d));
 s.on("error", reject);
 s.on("end", () => resolve(h.digest("hex")));
 });
}

function getPrimaryKeyColumns(tableName: string): s
 const info = getTableInfo(tableName);
 // pk is ordinal (1..n) for composite PK; 0 for n
 return info
 .filter((r) => r.pk && r.pk > 0)
 .sort((a, b) => a.pk - b.pk)
 .map((r) => r.name);
}
function getSinglePkColumnOrIdFallback(tableName: s

 if (pkCols.length === 1) return pkCols[0];

 if (cols.includes("id")) return "id";
 return null;
}

function canonicalize(v: any): any {
 if (v === undefined) return undefined;
 if (v === null) return null;
 if (typeof v === "number") {
 // normalize NaN/Infinity to null to avoid JSON
 if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return null;
 return v;
 }
 if (typeof v === "string" || typeof v === "boolea
 if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(canonicalize);
 if (typeof v === "object") {
 // If JSON column comes in as string, keep as s
 // If it is an object/array, canonicalize recur
1/6/26, 1:47 AM TRADE HUB - Platform Migration Design
https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-68c349da0e0c81918271a81f08092f44-trade-hub/c/694f12cc-34d8-832f-a3a6-e84c51a69f9b 146/1233
 const out: any = {};
 for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) {
 const cv = canonicalize(v[k]);
 if (cv === undefined) continue; // match JSON
 out[k] = cv;
 }
 return out;
 }
 // bigints/functions/symbols => string fallback
 try {
 return String(v);
 } catch {
 return null;
 }
}

}

async function sha256OfFile(filePath: string): Prom
 return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
 const s = fs.createReadStream(filePath);
 s.on("data", (d) => h.update(d));
 s.on("error", reject);
 s.on("end", () => resolve(h.digest("hex")));
 });
}

function pickChainOrderSql(table: string) {
 const cols = getTableColumns(table);
 const hasId = cols.includes("id");
 const hasCreatedAt = cols.includes("createdAt");
 const hasTs = cols.includes("ts");
 // Prefer deterministic monotonic id
 if (hasId) return `ORDER BY id ASC`;
 if (hasCreatedAt) return `ORDER BY createdAt ASC`
 if (hasTs) return `ORDER BY ts ASC`;
 return `ORDER BY rowid ASC`;
}

function canonicalize(v: any): any {
 if (v === undefined) return undefined;
 if (v === null) return null;
 if (typeof v === "number") {
 if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return null;
 return v;
 }
 if (typeof v === "string" || typeof v === "boolea
 if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(canonicalize);
 if (typeof v === "object") {
 const out: any = {};
 for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) {
1/6/26, 1:47 AM TRADE HUB - Platform Migration Design

 const cv = canonicalize(v[k]);
 if (cv === undefined) continue;
 out[k] = cv;
 }
 return out;
 }
 try {
 return String(v);
 } catch {
 return null;
 }
}

// ---------- Hash algorithm inference + recompute 
function pickChainOrderSql(table: string) {
 const cols = getTableColumns(table);
 const hasId = cols.includes("id");
 const hasCreatedAt = cols.includes("createdAt");
 const hasTs = cols.includes("ts");
 if (hasId) return `ORDER BY id ASC`;
 if (hasCreatedAt) return `ORDER BY createdAt ASC`
 if (hasTs) return `ORDER BY ts ASC`;
 return `ORDER BY rowid ASC`;
}

async function sha256OfFile(filePath: string): Prom
 return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
 const s = fs.createReadStream(filePath);
 s.on("data", (d) => h.update(d));
 s.on("error", reject);
 s.on("end", () => resolve(h.digest("hex")));
 });
}

function parseBool(v: any): boolean | null {

 }

}

 } catch {
 return null;
 }

4) What you now have operationally
Rotation workflow (admin)
1. POST /api/admin/migration-signing/generate with
{ activate:true, enforceImport:true }
2. Export jobs are now signed with the new key.
3. Import jobs enforce signature verification (if
enforceImport=true ).
Revocation workflow (admin)
1. POST /api/admin/migration-signing/revoke with {
keyId }
2. New imports signed with that key will fail
verification (trust set excludes it).
3. If revoked key was active signer, signing is autodisabled (safety default).

 const res = await fetch(url, {
 ...opts,

async function apiJson<T>(url: string, opts?: Reque
 const res = await fetch(url, {
 ...opts,
 headers: {
 "Content-Type": "application/json",
 ...(opts?.headers || {}),
 },
 });
 const txt = await res.text();
 let payload: any = null;
 try {
 payload = txt ? JSON.parse(txt) : null;
 } catch {
 payload = txt;
 }
 if (!res.ok) {
 const msg = payload?.error || payload?.message
 throw new Error(msg);
 }
 return payload as T;
}

function fmtBytes(n: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "—";
 const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
 let i = 0;
 let x = n;
 while (x >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) {
 x /= 1024;
 i++;
 }
 return `${x.toFixed(i === 0 ? 0 : 1)} ${u[i]}`;
}

export function primaryKeyColumns(table: string): s
 const info = tableInfo(table);
 const pk = info
 .filter((c) => c.pk > 0)
 .sort((a, b) => a.pk - b.pk)
 .map((c) => c.name);
 if (pk.length > 0) return pk;

 const cols = info.map((c) => c.name);
 if (cols.includes("id")) return ["id"];
 return [];
}

function dedupeEdges(edges: Edge[]): Edge[] {
 const seen = new Set<string>();
 const out: Edge[] = [];
 for (const e of edges) {
 const key = `${e.parentTable}.${e.parentCol} ->
 if (seen.has(key)) continue;
 seen.add(key);
 out.push(e);
 }

 out.sort((a, b) => {

 const kb = `${b.parentTable}.${b.parentCol}->${

 return ka.localeCompare(kb);
 });
 return out;
}
export function computeExportOrder(tables: string[]
 // parent before child (Kahn). Break ties determi
 const parents = new Map<string, Set<string>>(); /
 const children = new Map<string, Set<string>>(); 
 for (const t of tables) {
 parents.set(t, new Set());
 children.set(t, new Set());
 }
 for (const e of edges) {
 if (!tables.includes(e.parentTable) || !tables
 parents.get(e.childTable)!.add(e.parentTable);
 children.get(e.parentTable)!.add(e.childTable);
 }

 while (q.length) {

 order.push(n);

 }
 }
 // Cycle handling: append remaining deterministic

}

function canonicalize(v: any): any {
 if (v === undefined) return undefined;
 if (v === null) return null;
 if (typeof v === "number") return Number.isFinite
 if (typeof v === "string" || typeof v === "boolea
 if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(canonicalize);
 if (typeof v === "object") {
 const out: any = {};
 for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) {
 const cv = canonicalize(v[k]);
 if (cv === undefined) continue;
 out[k] = cv;
 }
 return out;
 }
 try {
 return String(v);
 } catch {
 return null;
 }
}

 }
 return null;
}

function dedupeEdges(edges: Edge[]): Edge[] {
 const seen = new Set<string>();
 const out: Edge[] = [];

 const kb = `${b.parentTable}.${b.parentCol}->${
 return ka.localeCompare(kb);
 });
 return out;
}

function computePreflightScore(p: UserBundlePreflig
 // Weighted, simple, stable.
 const presence = p.scoreBreakdown.presence;
 const reach = p.scoreBreakdown.reachability;
 const req = p.scoreBreakdown.requiredEdges;

 return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, s));
}

function fmtBytes(n: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "—";
 const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
 let i = 0;
 let x = n;
 while (x >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { x /= 1024
 return `${x.toFixed(i === 0 ? 0 : 1)} ${u[i]}`;
}

function fmtTs(ms: number | null | undefined) {

 try { return new Date(Number(ms)).toLocaleString(
}
function fmtBytes(n: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "—";
 const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
 let i = 0;
 let x = n;
 while (x >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { x /= 1024
 return `${x.toFixed(i === 0 ? 0 : 1)} ${u[i]}`;
}
function clamp01(n: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
 return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
}

1/6/26, 1:48 AM TRADE HUB - Platform Migration Design

Below are new files + full replacements and one small
patch to make the importer respect conflict policy.
0) What this adds (operationally)
A) Write Freeze (DB-level, no app-wide route
edits required)
Creates a migration_state table + SQLite triggers
on selected tables.
When freeze is enabled, any INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE
on those tables aborts with Migration freeze
active .
Toggle and table list are controlled via System
Config.
B) Watermark Capture (deterministic DELTA)
Computes MAX(updatedAt|ts|createdAt) per table
and stores a snapshot into system config as
migration.delta.lastWatermarkJson .

UI can “Capture watermark” then run a DELTA export
using that stored watermark.
C) Import Conflict Policy
Persisted policy: ERROR | SKIP | OVERWRITE
Import path should use INSERT vs INSERT OR
IGNORE vs INSERT OR REPLACE

function parseBool(v: any): boolean | null {
 if (v == null) return null;
 if (typeof v === "boolean") return v;
 if (typeof v === "number") return v !== 0;
 if (typeof v === "string") {
 const s = v.trim().toLowerCase();
 if (["1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"].includes(s)
 if (["0", "false", "no", "n", "off"].includes(s
 }
 return null;
}

 return null;

function asNumberMaybe(v: any): number | null {
 if (v == null) return null;
 if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) 
 if (typeof v === "string") {
 const s = v.trim();
 if (!s) return null;
 const n = Number(s);
 if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n;
 }
 return null;
}

1/6/26, 1:48 AM TRADE HUB - Platform Migration Design

Manual
1. Go to Admin → System Config → Migration.
2. Click Prepare cutover.
3. Confirm:
Freeze state shows freezeEnabled: true
Watermark snapshot exists
( lastWatermark.globalMaxMs )
Cutover token exists
4. Attempt to place a trade / update profile (any write)
→ should fail with Migration freeze active
(expected during cutover window).
5. Run an export job (USER_BUNDLE or
FULL_PLATFORM).
6. Mark export for cutover.
7. Run an import job:
First run with IMPORT should succeed.
Second run with same artifacts should skip
(artifact already imported).
8. Click Reconcile and confirm report output.
9. Click Thaw and confirm trading writes resume.

1/6/26, 1:48 AM TRADE HUB - Platform Migration Design

Next “PROCEED NEXT” (if you do it)
The next hardening step is the write-journal bridge
during freeze (for zero-downtime cutover):
During freeze, instead of failing writes, append
them to a journal table (orders/positions/trade
events) and replay them on the new platform after
import.
This is the standard approach if you cannot tolerate
a hard write halt.

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-68c349da0e0c81918271a81f08092f44-trade-hub/c/694f12cc-34d8-832f-a3a6-e84c51a69f9b 929/1233
proceeD Next
Thought for 2m 48s
Proceeding with the Write-Journal Bridge (zerodowntime cutover):
During cutover, writes are allowed (platform keeps
working).
Every DB write to selected tables is captured into an
append-only journal via SQLite triggers.
You export journal segments (NDJSON) and
import/replay them on the new platform after the
snapshot import.
Replay is idempotent (segment artifacts are hashdeduped, plus deterministic “upsert/delete by PK”
semantics).
This is the standard “snapshot + change stream replay”
migration pattern.

 const s = v.trim().toLowerCase();
 if (["1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"].includes(s)
 if (["0", "false", "no", "n", "off"].includes(s
 }
 return null;
}

 } catch {
 return null;
 }

function constantTimeEq(a: string, b: string): bool
 const ab = Buffer.from(String(a), "utf8");
 const bb = Buffer.from(String(b), "utf8");
 if (ab.length !== bb.length) return false;
 return crypto.timingSafeEqual(ab, bb);
}

function mulberry32(seed: number) {
 let t = seed >>> 0;
 return function () {
 t += 0x6D2B79F5;
 let x = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
 x ^= x + Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 7), 61 | x);
 return ((x ^ (x >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
 };
}
function hashSeedToU32(seed: string, salt: string)
 const h = sha256HexUtf8(`${seed}::${salt}`);
 return parseInt(h.slice(0, 8), 16) >>> 0;
}

 return !!row;

function mulberry32(seed: number) {
 let t = seed >>> 0;
 return function () {
 t += 0x6D2B79F5;
 let x = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
 x ^= x + Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 7), 61 | x);
 return ((x ^ (x >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
 };
}
function hashSeedToU32(seed: string, salt: string)
 const h = sha256HexUtf8(`${seed}::${salt}`);
 return parseInt(h.slice(0, 8), 16) >>> 0;
}

function isAdminUser(req: any): boolean {
 // Adjust to your auth model:
 // - if you use req.user.role or req.user.isAdmin
 const u = req.user;
 if (!u) return false;
 if (u.isAdmin) return true;
 const role = String(u.role ?? "").toUpperCase();
 return role === "ADMIN" || role === "SUPERADMIN";
}

 function mid(b: number, a: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(a))
 return (b + a) / 2;
 }
 function spread(b: number, a: number) {
 if (!Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(a))
 return Math.abs(a - b);
 }
}
